# DxUi Native Text Input Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use `superpowers:subagent-driven-development` or `superpowers:executing-plans` to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Remove the hidden DxUi text-input bridge window and replace it with a native retained-DxUi text input stack that keeps keyboard input, IME, UI Automation, NavigationView editing, password/secret entry, LTR/RTL, mixed BiDi text, color emoji, and performance coverage correct.

**Architecture:** `WindowHost` becomes the native Win32 focus/input owner for focused DxUi text controls. Text state stays in retained controls (`TextField`, editable `ComboBox`) and is exposed through a small `NativeTextInputSession` plus UI Automation `Text` / `TextEdit` provider support; the old bridge remains only as a temporary same-machine baseline until the native path passes the compatibility matrix. IME support must not rely on a hidden child edit HWND: the host must own caret geometry, composition/candidate placement, and text-store notifications directly.

**Tech Stack:** C++23, Win32, DirectWrite/Direct2D, IMM32, Text Services Framework (`msctf.h`, `textstor.h`), UI Automation (`uiautomationcore.h`), WIL RAII, `Debug::Perf`, `DxUiTests`, `--commands-selftest`, `Specs/TestRuns/`.

---

## Live Execution Instructions

- Keep this checklist at the beginning of the plan while the work is in progress.
- Before each code, test, spec, or perf slice, set the active checklist item to `[~]`.
- After fresh verification for that slice, change the item to `[x]` only when the tool output supports it.
- If coding uncovers a new problem, topic, risk, follow-up, or behavior rule, add it to **New Findings During Implementation** immediately and add or update the matching checklist item before continuing.
- Do not delete unchecked items; split broad items into smaller items when execution proves they are too large.

Status legend: `[ ]` not started, `[~]` in progress, `[x]` verified for this slice, `[blocked]` blocked with reason in the item text.

## New Findings During Implementation

- 2026-05-17: Direct PowerShell invocation of the GUI-subsystem `RedSalamander.exe` can return before command selftests finalize `results.json` and repository archives. Final selftest evidence from PowerShell must use `Start-Process -Wait -PassThru` or an equivalent `System.Diagnostics.Process` wait path.
- 2026-05-17: Production bridge deletion removed the `TextInputBackend::Bridge` enum value, bridge service window/WndProc implementation, bridge window messages, bridge state fields, and bridge audit allowlist entries. `SetTextInputBackend(...)` intentionally remains as a native-only compatibility setter for tests/debug plumbing until a broader API cleanup wants to remove the method itself.
- 2026-05-17: The only active test helper that still names `TextInputBridge` is `FindTextInputBridgeEdit(...)`, and it is intentionally retained as a negative sentinel so native tests can keep proving that no `DxUiTextInputBridgeWindow` child is created.
- 2026-05-17: `UI_DxUiSharedGrid.md` and `UI_VisibleTypographyAudit.md` still carried bridge-era normative wording after the code deletion; those authoritative specs now describe host-HWND native retained text input and historical-only bridge evidence.
- 2026-05-17: The DxUi component-test inventory in `Tests/README.md` had drifted to `709` while registered suite counts now total `629` after removing bridge-only hidden-window detail, context-menu duplicate, word-selection comparison, masked duplicate, editable ComboBox duplicate, single-line edit-routing probe, IME special-key, IME result-routing, IME window-anchoring, multiline edit-message/Return replacement, multiline blur/dialog-routing tests, and the final `TextInputBridge` compatibility suite. Native text-input closeout needs these counts current because `NativeTextInput`, `TextField`, and `ComboBox` are the bridge-removal safety net.
- 2026-05-17: Four `TextInputBridge` compatibility tests asserted hidden child bridge creation and bridge viewport sizing rather than user-visible behavior. The native suite already guards the replacement no-hidden-child contract, so those bridge-only detail tests were retired before production bridge deletion.
- 2026-05-17: Four more `TextInputBridge` context-menu tests proved multiline/wrapped `VK_APPS` and `Shift+F10` behavior through hidden-bridge special-key plumbing. Native host-HWND coverage already exercised the user-visible route, but the anchor-bounds assertion needed to move with it before retiring the bridge duplicates.
- 2026-05-17: Two single-line word-selection bridge tests used the hidden edit only to derive expected double-click/repeated-click ranges for the DxUi host. Native host-HWND tests now assert those punctuation-delimited ranges directly and verify typed replacement plus native-session state, so the bridge-comparison cases can be retired.
- 2026-05-17: Masked text bridge tests still duplicated native hidden masked copy/cut suppression and masked character input. Native coverage now explicitly checks accepted masked character input updates retained secret text while staying masked, so the hidden-bridge masked duplicates can be retired.
- 2026-05-17: Editable ComboBox still had bridge-only tests for exact-match item selection and delete/word-delete paths. Native host-HWND coverage now needs to own those user-visible combo semantics before the hidden bridge can be deleted.
- 2026-05-17: Several remaining single-line `TextInputBridge` tests exercise hidden-child edit compatibility (`WM_SETTEXT`, bridge-side caret reads) or duplicate native host routing for Tab, Enter, edit keys, undo, and paste. Native coverage should own the user-visible state changes, while hidden-child-only probes should be retired rather than carried into bridge deletion.
- 2026-05-17: IME special-key ownership is a native host-HWND contract, not a hidden bridge contract. The remaining bridge suite still duplicates single-line/multiline/wrapped Return/Escape/Tab ownership during active composition, so native multiline/wrapped breadth must be added before retiring those bridge variants.
- 2026-05-17: IME result-key routing has two durable states: result-only commits resume host/text key routing, while a result fragment plus continuing composition keeps Return/Escape/Tab owned by IME. Native host-HWND tests need to make that split explicit before bridge result-routing tests can be retired.
- 2026-05-17: During native IME result-routing migration, a first test attempt incorrectly expected `WM_KEYDOWN/VK_RETURN` alone to insert multiline text after a result-only commit. The durable contract is routing ownership: multiline Return must avoid the default button and stay focused/text-owned; text insertion belongs to the character input path.
- 2026-05-17: The next bridge IME block still proves composition/candidate window placement through the hidden edit HWND. The durable contract is host-owned IMM32 anchoring to native retained caret geometry, including multiline/wrapped visual lines and focused-control bounds changes while composition is active.
- 2026-05-17: The leading multiline bridge block still contains hidden-edit `WM_SETTEXT` and `EM_REPLACESEL` probes. Those messages are compatibility details of the temporary bridge HWND; the durable native contract is retained multiline text mutation through host-owned character, Return, clipboard/edit-message, TSF, and control APIs with LF-normalized retained state.
- 2026-05-17: The remaining multiline blur/dialog-flow bridge tests describe hidden child focus loss and bridge special-key forwarding. Native mode has no child edit HWND, so the durable contract is host-HWND focus-loss session teardown and retained multiline text controls owning Return while still allowing Tab/Shift+Tab traversal and Escape cancel routing.
- 2026-05-17: After the focused bridge-only retirements, the final 117 `TextInputBridge` cases no longer describe unique user-visible behavior. Their semantic coverage exists in `MultilineText`, `ReadOnly`, and `NativeTextInput`; their remaining unique assertions are hidden-child bridge selection/caret/viewport synchronization, so the suite can be retired before deleting the production bridge surface.
- 2026-05-16: Work started on branch `codex/dxui-native-text-input` because the checkout was on `master`.
- 2026-05-16: The existing bridge test inventory had 181 `Test...` functions in `Tests/DxUiTests/DxUiTests.TextInputBridge.cpp`; after removing the bridge-LOGFONT-only implementation probe, 180 behavior-oriented bridge compatibility cases remain.
- 2026-05-16: Native host focus initially reactivated the bridge because `WindowHost::OnSetFocus()` still unconditionally called `ActivateTextInputBridge(...)`; native/bridge backend routing must be applied in focus handlers, not only in `SetFocusControl(...)`.
- 2026-05-16: Host-owned `WM_CHAR` / `WM_KEYDOWN` already routed to retained controls, but native session state stayed stale unless the control explicitly called `SyncTextInputBridge(...)`. Native mode now syncs after handled host input and emits `dxui.textinput.key_to_state_us`.
- 2026-05-16: `NativeTextInputState::secretVisibleDotCount` started as scaffold-only and is now populated for focused masked `TextField` controls. Exact mode counts user-visible text elements, and concealed mode reports epoch-rotated privacy display counts.
- 2026-05-16: Native session lifecycle must mirror bridge lifecycle in root reset/prune paths. `ResetRootInteractionState()` and stale-control pruning still had bridge-specific teardown only, which can leave native session pointers/caret state stale after retained tree replacement.
- 2026-05-16: `WindowHost::SetFocusControl(...)` could re-focus a disabled/non-focusable control after stale-state pruning. Native text input now rejects retained-tree targets that are not effectively interactive and treats non-focusable focus targets as `nullptr`, preventing disabled text controls from immediately reactivating native sessions/carets.
- 2026-05-16: Single-line `TextField::OnBoundsChanged()` reset horizontal scroll but did not sync the focused native text session. Focused single-line bounds changes now refresh the native caret rect through the backend-neutral `SyncTextInput(...)` path.
- 2026-05-16: Win32 system caret ownership is thread/window global and has no project WIL wrapper. The native path confines `CreateCaret` / `ShowCaret` / `HideCaret` / `DestroyCaret` inside `WindowHost::NativeSystemCaret`, an internal RAII owner.
- 2026-05-16: Ordinary unmasked native clipboard and undo/redo routing already flows through retained `TextField` host-message handling. This slice needed native coverage only; masked, revealed, read-only, multiline, emoji, and BiDi transaction variants still need explicit native tests.
- 2026-05-16: Native `WM_SYSCHAR` still fell through to mnemonic handling instead of text input, while the bridge handled `WM_CHAR` and `WM_SYSCHAR` identically for printable text. Native mode now routes text-focused `WM_SYSCHAR` through retained `OnChar(...)` first and falls back to mnemonics only when the control does not consume it.
- 2026-05-16: Native host-owned text controls initially ignored direct edit messages (`WM_COPY`, `WM_CUT`, `WM_PASTE`, `WM_CLEAR`, `WM_UNDO`) that the hidden bridge used to own. Native mode now maps those host messages onto retained text-control edit commands and resynchronizes the native session after handled messages.
- 2026-05-16: Native `WM_CLEAR` must match bridge/Edit-control semantics: clear deletes an active selection only. The first native edit-message mapping used unmodified `VK_DELETE`, which can delete the next character at a collapsed caret, so no-selection clear now needs explicit regression coverage before the native bridge replacement can claim clipboard/edit parity.
- 2026-05-16: Native session state needs the focused control's inherited `FlowDirection` / DirectWrite reading direction even before full BiDi geometry exists. This is now captured in the session state; visual-order caret rectangles, hit-testing, TSF ACP mapping, IME candidate placement, and UIA ranges remain separate BiDi work.
- 2026-05-16: Inherited `FlowDirection` changes after focus initially left the native session cache stale because flow invalidation only repainted controls. Focused text controls now resync through `WindowHost::SyncTextInput(...)` when their effective flow direction changes, keeping native state/caret refresh backend-neutral for `TextField` and editable `ComboBox`.
- 2026-05-16: Editable `ComboBox` is a first-class native text-input consumer, not just a TextField follow-up. Current native coverage proves editable-combo focus uses the host/native session without creating the hidden bridge child, mirrors typed text/caret state, and refreshes inherited RTL metadata through the same backend-neutral sync path.
- 2026-05-16: Native mode initially ignored host-HWND IME lifecycle messages; only the hidden bridge tracked composition ownership. `WindowHost` now records native composition start/end state directly, clears conversion-target ranges on end/result-only composition, emits `dxui.textinput.ime_update_us`, and keeps Return/Escape/Tab on the active native composition instead of routing them to dialog/default focus paths.
- 2026-05-16: Read-only native text controls initially still accepted native IME start messages into `NativeTextInputState`. The native IME handler now treats read-only state as composition-suppressed, clears composition/conversion ranges, and records the suppression through the same `dxui.textinput.ime_update_us` metric.
- 2026-05-16: Native IME start initially collapsed active selections to an empty caret composition range. IME start now anchors the native composition range to the selected logical UTF-16 span when a selection is active, leaving full replacement/result commit behavior for the later payload-retrieval slice.
- 2026-05-16: A no-payload `WM_IME_COMPOSITION` without an active native composition initially created an empty composition range. Native mode now treats that message as a no-op/suppression when there is no active composition, while preserving an already active composition for later no-GCS caret/candidate updates.
- 2026-05-16: Native IME lifecycle initially left IMM32 composition and candidate UI at default host-window placement. Native mode now uses a WIL-scoped `ImmGetContext` / `ImmReleaseContext` pair and updates `ImmSetCompositionWindow` / `ImmSetCandidateWindow` from the host-owned native caret rect on IME start/composition messages; scroll, DPI, and layout-driven refresh remain separate geometry work.
- 2026-05-16: Deterministic native IME payload tests cannot rely on the machine's active OS IME. The native handler now reads real IMM32 payloads in production and exposes a test-only injected payload hook so `GCS_RESULTSTR`, `GCS_COMPSTR`, attributes, clauses, cursor position, result commit, active preview, and cancel-restore behavior stay covered in CI.
- 2026-05-17: `NativeTextInputState` now exposes active IME diagnostics for `GCS_CURSORPOS` and `GCS_COMPCLAUSE` as absolute retained-text indexes (`compositionCursorIndex` and `compositionClauseBoundaries`), and clears them with composition end/cancel.
- 2026-05-17: Native IME result payloads after a live preview must resolve replacement against the original IME base selection/caret range, not the preview-length active span. Multiline/wrapped preview-then-result coverage now guards this so result commits do not delete unrelated base text after the anchor.
- 2026-05-17: Editable `ComboBox` IME candidate placement needs the same bounds-change reanchoring contract as `TextField`. `TextField::OnBoundsChanged()` already resyncs focused native text input, but editable `ComboBox` currently has no bounds-change hook, so active IMM32 composition/candidate windows can remain anchored to stale caret geometry after layout moves the focused combo.
- 2026-05-17: Native IME previews already mutated retained text and exposed composition/conversion target ranges, but paint/debug state did not style those ranges. `TextField` and editable `ComboBox` paint now share retained range-derived composition and target underline rectangles so active IME previews stay visible without hidden native text painting.
- 2026-05-17: Public retained setters can move focused text/caret state outside key or pointer handlers. `TextField::SetText(...)`, `TextField::SetSelectionRange(...)`, `ComboBox::SetText(...)`, and `ComboBox::SetEditableSelectionRange(...)` now resync focused native sessions so active IME composition/candidate windows follow programmatic caret moves without caller-owned manual sync.
- 2026-05-17: Focused text geometry can also move through style/layout setters rather than text edits. `TextField` padding setters alter the native text viewport directly, and editable `ComboBox` density changes alter its edit insets/drop-button width; both need active native text-input resync so IME composition/candidate windows do not stay on stale caret geometry.
- 2026-05-17: The authoritative density table already required compact `TextField` default vertical padding to shrink from 4 DIP to 2 DIP, but the retained `TextField` implementation still used fixed padding unless callers set it manually. Compact density needs to affect the default text viewport while preserving explicit caller padding.
- 2026-05-17: Multiline and single-line mixed-BiDi UIA selected-range rectangles can use retained DirectWrite `HitTestTextRange` geometry while preserving logical UTF-16 range text; broader per-glyph/per-range BiDi policy still needs more breadth.
- 2026-05-17: The retained single-line DirectWrite range-rectangle hook was implemented for `TextField` first, but editable `ComboBox` shares the same UIA `TextPattern` promise and needs its own editable-text-range override so selected mixed-BiDi ComboBox ranges do not fall back to conservative viewport geometry.
- 2026-05-17: UIA text/text-edit event coverage needs deterministic observability in the component harness. Native text-input event counters now track provider-event attempts for retained text changes, retained selection changes, retained caret/active-text-position changes, IME composition text changes, and IME conversion-target changes while production raises the matching UI Automation events.
- 2026-05-16: Native IME window placement must refresh from every native caret geometry update, not just from IME messages. Focused field movement during an active native composition now re-applies IMM32 composition/candidate placement from the refreshed caret rect.
- 2026-05-16: Masked `TextField` UIA previously exposed no `IsPassword` state and could leak the secret through ValuePattern. The provider now reports masked fields as password fields, preserves explicit accessible names, and returns an empty value for masked text.
- 2026-05-16: Native IME DPI-change reanchoring was already covered by the existing DPI/caret refresh path after the moved-field fix, but lacked a deterministic native regression. The new test pins composition/candidate placement to the scaled native caret client rect after `WM_DPICHANGED`.
- 2026-05-16: Existing single-line edit helpers only protected UTF-16 surrogate pairs, so Backspace/Delete could split user-visible emoji clusters such as `woman technologist` ZWJ sequences, variation-selector emoji, skin-tone modifier emoji, and regional-indicator flags. Plain caret movement and Backspace/Delete now use shared text-element boundaries for retained `TextField`, editable `ComboBox`, and native-session state sync while storage remains logical UTF-16.
- 2026-05-16: Masked emoji color-font tests should compare the masked and unmasked pixel signal in the same window rather than requiring zero warm pixels; focus chrome and unrelated themed pixels can be warm even when the underlying emoji is replaced by bullet display text.
- 2026-05-16: DirectWrite point hit-testing can return a caret index inside a regional-indicator flag. Shared single-line hit-testing now snaps raw hit-test results to the nearest text-element boundary before retained controls or native session state accept pointer placement.
- 2026-05-16: UIA `ISelectionProvider::GetSelection` and `ITextProvider::GetSelection` share the same COM method signature. The provider now branches text controls to `ITextRangeProvider` arrays while leaving Tree/Grid selection-provider arrays unchanged.
- 2026-05-16: Editable `ComboBox` must expose UIA text ranges from its editable text, not only ValuePattern/displayed selection text, so assistive clients can read and track native edit composition consistently with `TextField`.
- 2026-05-16: The first TSF slice was intentionally direct-test-only: `DebugCreateNativeTextInputTextStoreForTest()` created an `ITextStoreACP` for the focused native text control before the store was registered with a TSF document manager/context during focus activation. The 2026-05-17 TSF activation/composition/ACP2 slices now create and tear down the thread manager/document manager/context for focused native text controls, expose `ITfContextOwnerCompositionSink`, and expose `ITextStoreACP2` over the same retained ACP store.
- 2026-05-17: The SDK `ITextStoreACP2` surface is the same ACP text/selection/geometry contract used by the current native store except it omits `GetWnd`; exposing it over the same retained store implementation is low-risk compatibility coverage and closes the lingering TSF optional-interface blocker before bridge deletion.
- 2026-05-16: Concealed password masking now uses a per-field epoch cache instead of a fixed upper-bound bucket. It preserves same-bucket stability while regenerating the display count on focus entry, explicit full reset, policy/mask changes, and bucket-boundary crossings.
- 2026-05-16: Visible reveal state now intentionally permits Copy/Cut/Paste through the retained native path, while UIA still suppresses masked text via `IsPassword` regardless of reveal state. Blur/read-only/disabled/Escape-before-cancel remask plus pointer and keyboard reveal-eye peek are covered; remaining command-surface remask triggers are still separate work before bridge removal.
- 2026-05-16: Secret reveal mode and transient reveal state are separate policies. `PasswordRevealMode::Peek` keeps the existing press-and-hold eye affordance, `PasswordRevealMode::Hidden` removes pointer/keyboard reveal affordances, and `PasswordRevealMode::Visible` is a caller-selected plaintext policy that remains visible across blur/read-only/disabled transitions until changed.
- 2026-05-16: Escape routing for visible masked native fields must hide the secret before invoking the host cancel button. `TextField::OnKeyDown(...)` now remasks and syncs native state for Escape while returning `false`, preserving the existing WindowHost cancel path.
- 2026-05-16: `Control::SetEnabled(...)` did not previously have a control-specific transition hook. Native password remasking now uses a no-op base `OnEnabledChanged(...)` hook that lets `TextField` hide a revealed secret immediately when disabled without changing other controls' enabled behavior.
- 2026-05-16: Masked `TextField` previously reused the clear-button slot, so clicking the trailing button could erase a secret instead of peeking it. Masked single-line fields now use a mutually exclusive eye button in that slot; pointer and keyboard access are covered, while UIA exposure remains open.
- 2026-05-16: UIA text ranges initially returned no bounding rectangles. The first native range-geometry slice now returns a conservative screen-space control rectangle for non-empty `TextField` ranges; precise glyph, multiline/wrapped, RTL, and mixed-BiDi rectangles remain open.
- 2026-05-16: UIA text ranges initially could not move by character, word, or line. The provider now supports text-element-aware character-unit endpoint/range movement, including a ZWJ emoji cluster as one user-visible character, word-unit endpoint/collapsed/noncollapsed selected-range movement, logical line endpoint/selected-range movement for newline-delimited multiline `TextField` content, and wrapped visual-line endpoint/selected-range movement for multiline `TextField` content when native caret geometry proves wrapping beyond logical lines; fuller emoji-family range breadth and BiDi-aware geometry remain open.
- 2026-05-16: UIA text range `Select()` initially succeeded without mutating the retained control, and then still mutated retained controls directly when called from a non-window thread. It now dispatches `TextField` and editable `ComboBox` range selection back through the host accessibility action message, applies retained selection state on the host window thread, and resyncs active native/bridge text state.
- 2026-05-16: UIA `RangeFromPoint` initially returned the whole document range. It now returns a collapsed caret range for `TextField` and editable `ComboBox` points using the shared DirectWrite hit-test path; multiline/wrapped and BiDi visual-point coverage remain open.
- 2026-05-16: UIA range clones initially compared unequal because `Compare` used COM pointer identity and `CompareEndpoints` always returned equality. Text ranges now compare same-provider HWND/path/span semantics and endpoint offsets; cross-provider and multiline/RTL endpoint semantics remain to be broadened with later geometry work.
- 2026-05-16: NavigationView address/full-path editing still owned Enter/Escape/Tab, autosuggest, path-segment Ctrl+Backspace, and newline-stripping paste by subclassing the hidden DxUi bridge HWND. NavigationView now creates its `NavigationDxTextHost` in native text-input mode, attaches policy to native DxUi callbacks, uses shared retained `TextField` editing for Ctrl+Backspace and single-line paste normalization, and keeps only the small visible-host edit-message compatibility surface (`WM_GETTEXT*`, `WM_SETTEXT`, `EM_GETSEL`, `EM_SETSEL`, `EM_REPLACESEL`) for command selftests.
- 2026-05-16: NavigationView debug/selftest state still carried bridge-era names after moving to native text input. The public snapshot now exposes `currentEditInputHwnd` as the backend-neutral active input HWND, and `NavigationDxTextHost::GetBridgeHwnd()` has been removed in favor of `GetTextInputHwnd()`.
- 2026-05-16: NavigationView command selftests also need backend-neutral caret and composition diagnostics, not only host/input HWND readiness. `NavigationViewDebugSnapshot` now exposes `currentEditCaretScreenRectValid`, `currentEditCaretScreenRect`, `currentEditHasActiveComposition`, `currentEditCompositionStart`, and `currentEditCompositionEnd`, and the path-region keyboard activation selftest drives `WM_IME_STARTCOMPOSITION` / `WM_IME_ENDCOMPOSITION` against the native host to validate active/cleared composition state.
- 2026-05-16: NavigationView edit-mode Enter/Escape/Tab/autosuggest policy still ran in the host WndProc hook before DxUi's native IME ownership could see active composition. Enter now flows through `TextField::SetOnSubmitted(...)`, Escape through `WindowHost::SetOnEscape(...)`, Tab through `WindowHost::SetOnTabBoundary(...)`, and autosuggest Up/Down through `TextField::SetOnPreviewKeyDown(...)`; native composition now owns arrow/navigation edit keys before those callbacks. Path Ctrl+Backspace and newline-filtered paste no longer live in the hook; they use shared native `TextField` path-aware word deletion and single-line paste normalization.
- 2026-05-16: NavigationView invalid-path feedback still used native edit balloon tips, which tied validation feedback to the bridge/edit-control path. Invalid address-bar and full-path popup values now keep edit mode active, focus the native DxUi text host, set retained `TextField` HelpText for UIA, and show a DxUi-owned tooltip instead of sending `EM_SHOWBALLOONTIP`.
- 2026-05-16: Address-bar clipboard/edit compatibility paths still called the bridge-named `WindowHost::SyncTextInputBridge(...)` directly after retained `TextField` edits. `WindowHost::SyncTextInput(...)` is now the backend-neutral sync API, NavigationView uses it for command and edit-message compatibility, and the old bridge-named method was removed in the later wrapper-deletion slice.
- 2026-05-16: Some production dialogs still called the bridge-named `WindowHost::CommitFocusedTextInputBridge(...)` even though the method already handled native sessions. The API is now `CommitFocusedTextInput(...)`, and production/app callers plus semantic tests use the backend-neutral name.
- 2026-05-16: Retained-control text state still used bridge-era public names even after the native session became the production path. `TextInputState`, `ExportTextInputState(...)`, `ImportTextInputState(...)`, and `TryReadTextInputState(...)` are now the backend-neutral state contract; bridge-specific implementation helpers remain tracked under the final deletion task.
- 2026-05-16: The compatibility wrapper `WindowHost::SyncTextInputBridge(...)` was still public after production call sites moved to `SyncTextInput(...)`. The wrapper is now removed, and legacy semantic tests call the backend-neutral sync API directly.
- 2026-05-16: The DxUi text-input capability name still exposed the old bridge implementation detail. Inventory found only the `Control` base plus `TextField` and editable `ComboBox` overrides; the API is now backend-neutral `SupportsTextInput()`, while bridge-state import/export method names remain tracked under the final bridge deletion task.
- 2026-05-16: Native secret reveal must treat host/app deactivation and pointer-capture loss as disclosure-ending events. `WM_ACTIVATE/WA_INACTIVE` and `WM_ACTIVATEAPP/FALSE` now run the host text-input focus-loss teardown, and `TextField::OnCaptureLost(...)` clears the reveal-button press latch, remasks, and syncs native state.
- 2026-05-16: Hidden single-line concealed password fields should avoid pointer-per-character disclosure. Pointer focus now clears selection and snaps the logical caret to the retained secret end, while keyboard editing, command selection, paste, undo, and redo continue to operate on the secret text.
- 2026-05-16: The reveal eye is a virtual UIA child of the owning `TextField`, not a retained `Button` control. It resolves through the field's accessibility path, reports a configurable `Button`/`InvokePattern` name, exposes the eye-slot rectangle, and still leaves masked Value/TextPattern content empty after Invoke.
- 2026-05-16: Editable `ComboBox` UIA text ranges must use the editable text viewport and retained edit selection, not the full combo hit bounds or displayed value fallback. `ComboBox` now exposes retained editable selection setters/getters and its own editable hit-test hook so UIA `RangeFromPoint`, `GetSelection`, and range `Select()` round-trip through the same retained state as native editing.
- 2026-05-16: The direct TSF store initially treated editable `ComboBox` text as collapsed-at-end when native state was unavailable, and `ApplyState(...)` lost TSF selection by calling `ComboBox::SetText(...)` without restoring the requested caret/range. The store now exports retained ComboBox selection and reapplies `SetEditableSelectionRange(...)` after TSF text/selection changes.
- 2026-05-16: TSF `InsertTextAtSelection(TS_IAS_QUERYONLY)` initially returned projected ACP bounds but left `TS_TEXTCHANGE` zeroed. Query-only insert now reports the same projected start/old-end/new-end span that a mutating insert would use, without changing retained text or notifying sinks.
- 2026-05-16: Collapsing a focused native `TextField` to empty bounds exposed two missing layout guards: native caret geometry could ask `TextField::GetTextInputBridgeCaretRect(...)` to clamp against an invalid text rectangle, and TSF `GetTextExt(...)` could do the same instead of reporting unavailable layout. Empty text rectangles now clear native caret geometry and TSF `GetTextExt`, `GetScreenExt`, and `GetACPFromPoint` return `TS_E_NOLAYOUT`.
- 2026-05-16: Caret geometry is a retained-control/native-session contract rather than a bridge contract. `Control::GetTextInputCaretRect(...)` is now the backend-neutral API used by native and compatibility paths, and the old bridge-named wrapper was removed after callers moved.
- 2026-05-16: Text-input viewport geometry is also a retained-control contract. `Control::GetTextInputViewportRect(...)` is now the backend-neutral API, and the old bridge-named wrapper was removed after callers moved.
- 2026-05-16: The bridge-named text-input caret/viewport compatibility wrappers no longer have callers after the backend-neutral geometry migration. `Control::GetTextInputBridgeCaretRect(...)` and `GetTextInputBridgeViewportRect(...)` are now deleted; `GetTextInputCaretRect(...)` and `GetTextInputViewportRect(...)` are the only retained-control geometry APIs.
- 2026-05-16: HWND focus-sensitive DxUi suites such as `NativeTextInput` and `TextInputBridge` must run serially for closeout evidence. Running both as parallel foreground-window peers can make focus assertions observe the other suite's real test window.
- 2026-05-16: Active text-input HWND/debug state should not force bridge terminology. `WindowHost::HasActiveTextInput()` and `WindowHost::GetTextInputHwnd()` now report the active native host HWND or compatibility bridge HWND as appropriate, and the bridge-specific public wrappers `HasActiveTextInputBridge()` / `GetTextInputBridgeHwnd()` are deleted. Compatibility tests that still prove hidden-child behavior now combine explicit child-window lookup helpers with the backend-neutral diagnostics.
- 2026-05-16: Production retained-control sync call sites should use `WindowHost::SyncTextInput(...)`, not bridge vocabulary. Common controls, TSF/UIA integration, app DxUi edit hosts, and legacy semantic tests now call the backend-neutral sync API, and the bridge-named public wrapper is deleted.
- 2026-05-16: The `DebugGetNonVisibleTextServiceBridgeFont(...)` hook and its single bridge-LOGFONT test were pure hidden-bridge implementation coverage. They are now removed; the remaining bridge suite keeps behavior compatibility coverage while final bridge deletion work continues.
- 2026-05-16: The hidden compatibility bridge did not need a private font bridge once the LOGFONT test hook was deleted. `Common/DxUi/DxUi.TextInput.cpp` no longer creates a non-visible bridge `HFONT`, sends `WM_SETFONT`, includes `DxUi.Typography`, or carries the DxUi text-service font allowlist rows.
- 2026-05-16: The backend feature gate has flipped: `WindowHost` now defaults to native text input, and the surviving `TextInputBridge` compatibility suite explicitly opts into `TextInputBackend::Bridge` before asserting hidden-child bridge behavior. This keeps production/default coverage on the native path while the final bridge deletion task ports or retires the remaining bridge-named behavior inventory.
- 2026-05-16: `DxUiTests.ReadOnly.cpp` still carried a local bridge opt-in host wrapper after the default flipped to native. The ReadOnly suite now uses the default/native attached host for logical and wrapped multiline read-only edit-message coverage, asserts no hidden bridge child, and validates host-HWND/native state through backend-neutral diagnostics.
- 2026-05-16: Multiline and wrapped multiline context-menu keyboard parity was still represented by direct retained-control tests and the bridge compatibility suite. `NativeTextInput` now covers attached native host-HWND routing for `VK_APPS` and `Shift+F10` on logical and wrapped multiline fields without creating a hidden bridge child.
- 2026-05-16: Focus activation/deactivation still had repeated native-vs-bridge branches in `WindowHost::SetFocusControl(...)`, `OnSetFocus()`, `OnKillFocus()`, root reset, and backend switching. Those paths now call backend-neutral private `ActivateTextInput(...)` / `DeactivateTextInput(...)` dispatch helpers, leaving explicit bridge calls concentrated in bridge implementation and compatibility-only paths.
- 2026-05-16: `WindowHost::TryReadTextInputState(...)` had a backend-neutral name but still read only the compatibility bridge cache. It now projects the active native session into `TextInputState`, and `NativeTextInput` covers the default native path through the backend-neutral reader.
- 2026-05-16: Connection Manager command selftests still exposed bridge-era text-input naming even though the helper already returned `WindowHost::GetTextInputHwnd()`. The debug APIs and masked-secret command case now use backend-neutral/native names, and the double-click word-selection command uses retained DxUi caret geometry/selection instead of hidden-edit `EM_*` probes.
- 2026-05-17: Single-line double-click word-selection parity was still represented by bridge-named host/hidden-edit comparison tests. `NativeTextInput` now covers attached native host-HWND `WM_LBUTTONDBLCLK` and synthesized repeated-click word selection on a host class without `CS_DBLCLKS`, including punctuation-delimited text, no hidden bridge child, and native-session state sync.
- 2026-05-17: Native pointer coverage now includes attached host-HWND third-click select-all and drag-selection replacement for single-line `TextField`, keeping retained selection and `NativeTextInputState` synchronized without creating a hidden bridge child.
- 2026-05-17: Mixed-BiDi drag selection exposed that the shared single-line helpers were still treating layout/caret/hit-test geometry as LTR-width-only. The current slice now routes effective DirectWrite reading direction and bounded layout width through single-line rendering, caret measurement, hit-testing, selection paint, native TSF point/extents, and UIA `RangeFromPoint` fallback, while preserving retained selection and clipboard text in logical UTF-16 order.
- 2026-05-17: Correct RTL single-line layout changed the mixed-BiDi emoji rendering fixture's visible region; the existing capture put the color emoji run off the test bitmap. The fixture now uses a narrower visible field and fewer emoji so the color-font assertion still proves rendered warm color pixels instead of testing clipped-away content.
- 2026-05-17: The first mixed-BiDi pointer matrix oracle used ideal floating-point DirectWrite caret positions, but host input receives pixel-rounded coordinates. The test now snaps the probe point through the host DPI conversion before asking DirectWrite for the expected logical caret index, matching the real `WM_LBUTTONDOWN` point.
- 2026-05-16: UIA `TextRange::Move(TextUnit_Word, ...)` for a noncollapsed selected range initially reported no movement. Noncollapsed word movement now advances the range start through the existing word-boundary helper and expands to the next word span, trimming trailing separator whitespace from the exposed range text.
- 2026-05-16: UIA `TextRange::Move(TextUnit_Line, ...)` and line endpoint movement for multiline ranges initially reported no movement. The line slices now move endpoints and selected ranges across logical newline-delimited lines and wrapped visual lines, exposing destination line text without newline separators for logical lines and native-caret-derived spans for wrapped lines.
- 2026-05-16: Multiline `TextField` UIA should use `TextPattern` as the content contract and should not expose `ValuePattern`. The provider now keeps ValuePattern compatibility scoped to single-line `TextField` and editable `ComboBox`.
- 2026-05-16: Editable `ComboBox` command parity needs its own retained edit history, not just native-session sync. Ctrl+X, Shift+Delete, and Ctrl+Z/Y initially had no retained ComboBox path, so native command coverage now guards copy/cut/paste, undo/redo, normalized paste, popup keyboard open/close, retained selection restore, and native-state resync together.
- 2026-05-17: The broad remaining-Win32-UI audit exposed a FolderWindow pane command-line blocker that an older Done plan had already identified: `RedSalamander/FolderWindow.FileSystem.Navigation.Part.cpp` still created visible native `STATIC` / `EDIT` controls and applied `DEFAULT_GUI_FONT` through `WM_SETFONT`. The command-line input now uses a `FolderWindow` DxUi host plus native-backend retained `TextField`, and the command selftest snapshot guards `usesDxUiHost`, `usesNativeTextInput`, and zero visible native child controls.
- 2026-05-17: The same audit exposed an allowlist drift row in `RedSalamander/FolderView.Icons.cpp`: `ENABLE_TESTS` synthetic thumbnail generation uses `GetDC(nullptr)` only to create deterministic DIB thumbnails. This is now explicitly allowlisted as test-only bitmap interop with an exit condition to replace it if a non-HDC bitmap factory helper lands.

---

## Research References

- Microsoft Learn, [Text Services Framework](https://learn.microsoft.com/windows/desktop/TSF/text-services-framework): TSF is the Windows framework for advanced text input, multilingual input processors, handwriting, and speech; the native DxUi path should integrate at this layer rather than delegating to a hidden edit child forever.
- Microsoft Learn, [ITextStoreACP](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/win32/api/textstor/nn-textstor-itextstoreacp): the application implements this interface so TSF can read, select, and mutate an ACP-indexed text store.
- Microsoft Learn, [ITextStoreACP::GetACPFromPoint](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/win32/api/textstor/nf-textstor-itextstoreacp-getacpfrompoint): native hit testing must convert screen points into text positions; DxUi can back this with existing DirectWrite hit-test helpers.
- Microsoft Learn, [WM_IME_COMPOSITION](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/win32/intl/wm-ime-composition): a window that displays composition characters itself must process the message and retrieve composition data instead of relying on default edit-control behavior.
- Microsoft Learn, [TextEdit Control Pattern](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/win32/winauto/textedit-control-pattern): controls that enable IME composition should expose active composition and conversion target ranges through UI Automation.
- Microsoft Learn, [Edit Control Type](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/win32/winauto/uiauto-supporteditcontroltype): edit controls must support UIA Text pattern; the `Name` must not be the field value.
- Microsoft Learn, [About the Text and TextRange Control Patterns](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/win32/winauto/uiauto-about-text-and-textrange-patterns): UIA Edit and Document controls must support `ITextProvider` / `ITextRangeProvider`; text controls with selection/caret must expose selection.
- Microsoft Learn, [Implementing the UI Automation Value Control Pattern](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/dotnet/framework/ui-automation/implementing-the-ui-automation-value-control-pattern): single-line edit controls may keep `ValuePattern`; multiline edit controls should expose content through `TextPattern`.
- Microsoft Learn, [UiaReturnRawElementProvider](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/win32/api/uiautomationcoreapi/nf-uiautomationcoreapi-uiareturnrawelementprovider): custom HWND providers must answer `WM_GETOBJECT` and notify UIA on destroy.
- Microsoft Learn, [About Carets](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/win32/menurc/about-carets): Windows has one system caret per message queue; custom text controls should create, move, show, hide, and destroy the caret only while they own focus.
- Microsoft Learn, [DirectWrite Hit Testing](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/win32/directwrite/how-to-perform-hit-testing-on-a-text-layout): `IDWriteTextLayout::HitTestPoint` and related hit-test APIs are the right basis for pointer-to-text-position mapping.

## Current Code Truth

- `Common/DxUi/DxUi.TextInput.cpp` now contains backend-neutral retained text-state helpers over the native session cache; it no longer owns `DxUiTextInputBridgeWindow`, `EnsureTextInputBridge(...)`, `DestroyTextInputBridge(...)`, bridge WndProc routing, bridge-side edit-message emulation, bridge undo/redo vectors, or bridge state import/export.
- `Common/DxUi/DxUi.NativeTextInput.cpp` owns the production native host-HWND text-input session activation, deactivation, IMM32/IME handling, TSF focus/context wiring, and native session synchronization for focused `TextField` and editable `ComboBox` controls.
- `Common/DxUi/DxUi.h` exposes only `TextInputBackend::Native` plus backend-neutral `TextInputState`, `SupportsTextInput()`, `Control::ExportTextInputState(...)` / `ImportTextInputState(...)`, `WindowHost::TryReadTextInputState(...)`, `WindowHost::CommitFocusedTextInput(...)`, `WindowHost::SyncTextInput(...)`, `WindowHost::HasActiveTextInput()`, `WindowHost::GetTextInputHwnd()`, `Control::GetTextInputViewportRect(...)`, `Control::GetTextInputCaretRect(...)`, and native-session debug helpers.
- `Common/DxUi/DxUi.WindowHost.cpp` routes host-HWND focus, keyboard, IME, reset, and teardown paths directly through the native text-input session; it no longer treats a bridge child HWND as a focus target or receives `WndMsg::kDxUiTextInputBridge*` messages.
- `RedSalamander/NavigationView.Edit.cpp` attaches address-bar policy to the native DxUi text stack: Enter/Escape/Tab use `TextField`/`WindowHost` callbacks, autosuggest Up/Down uses `TextField::SetOnPreviewKeyDown(...)`, Ctrl+Backspace path deletion and newline-filtered paste use shared retained `TextField` behavior, and the visible host hook is limited to edit-message compatibility (`WM_GETTEXT*`, `WM_SETTEXT`, `EM_GETSEL`, `EM_SETSEL`, `EM_REPLACESEL`) while the host uses the native DxUi text-input backend.
- `RedSalamander/NavigationViewInternal.h` exposes `NavigationDxTextHost::GetTextInputHwnd()` for debug snapshots and selftests; in native mode this resolves to the visible host HWND rather than a hidden bridge child, and `NavigationViewDebugSnapshot::currentEditInputHwnd` is the backend-neutral command/selftest field.
- `Tests/DxUiTests/DxUiTests.TextInputBridge.cpp` is deleted, `DxUiTests.cpp` no longer registers `--suite=TextInputBridge`, and `DxUiTests.vcxproj` no longer compiles the bridge-suite source. The retained semantic coverage that used to justify the compatibility suite now lives in `NativeTextInput`, `MultilineText`, `ReadOnly`, `TextField`, `ComboBox`, `Accessibility`, and `WindowHost`.
- `Specs/UI/UI_NavigationView.md`, `Specs/Testing/Testing_SelfTests.md`, `Specs/UI/UI_VisibleNativeAudit.md`, `Specs/UI/UI_VisibleTypographyAudit.md`, `Specs/UI/UI_DxUiSharedGrid.md`, `Specs/UI/UI_DxUiWinUIDesign.md`, `Specs/Testing/Testing_TestCoverage.md`, and `Tests/README.md` now document the host-HWND native retained text-input contract and current bridge-retirement evidence.

## Non-Negotiable Exit Criteria

- No production code creates or looks up `DxUiTextInputBridgeWindow`.
- No `WindowHost` text input path requires a hidden child edit HWND, hidden zero-region window, `WM_SETFONT`, or bridge `LOGFONTW` debug hook.
- `TextField` and editable `ComboBox` still pass all keyboard, pointer, clipboard, undo/redo, read-only, masked, multiline, wrapped multiline, surrogate-pair, and composition coverage previously represented by the retired `TextInputBridge` suite.
- LTR, RTL, and mixed BiDi text work in `TextField`, editable `ComboBox`, and NavigationView edit hosts: visual text order, caret geometry, selection painting, pointer hit-testing, deletion, clipboard ranges, TSF ACP ranges, IME candidate placement, and UIA text ranges must all stay consistent.
- Color emoji and other color-font glyphs render in native color through DirectWrite color-font drawing in normal text, selected text, multiline text, editable `ComboBox`, and RTL/mixed-BiDi text. The native path must not regress to monochrome text-brush rendering.
- Password/secret fields support complete masked-entry behavior: one-dot-per-grapheme feedback, optional length-hiding dot display, press-and-hold eye reveal, keyboard-accessible reveal, paste while hidden, copy/cut/paste while intentionally revealed, backspace/delete, undo/redo, no UIA value exposure, `IsPassword=true`, and automatic re-mask on blur, disable, or teardown.
- NavigationView address-bar edit and full-path popup edit keep Enter/Escape/Tab, autosuggest, Ctrl+Backspace, paste filtering, invalid-path feedback, and pane-level clipboard command routing.
- UIA edit controls expose correct `Edit` control type, non-value `Name`, `ValuePattern` where appropriate, `TextPattern` for all edit controls, `TextEditPattern` when composition is supported, selection/caret ranges, password state for masked fields, and correct cross-thread dispatch.
- Perf evidence exists for activation, key-to-visible update, composition update, text layout/hit-test, and UIA text-range queries, with baseline and candidate archives under `Specs/TestRuns/`.
- `Specs/UI/UI_NavigationView.md`, `Specs/UI/UI_DxUiWinUIDesign.md`, `Specs/UI/UI_DxUiSharedGrid.md`, `Specs/UI/UI_VisibleNativeAudit.md`, `Specs/UI/UI_VisibleTypographyAudit.md`, `Specs/Testing/Testing_SelfTests.md`, `Specs/Testing/Testing_TestCoverage.md`, and `Tests/README.md` are updated before moving this plan to `Specs/Plans/Done/`.

## Protected Scenarios And Metrics

| Scenario | Metric Family | Deterministic Gate |
|----------|---------------|--------------------|
| Focus a single-line `TextField` and type printable text | `dxui.textinput.activate_us`, `dxui.textinput.key_to_state_us`, `dxui.textinput.key_to_paint_us` | `DxUiTests.exe --suite=NativeTextInput` |
| Edit multiline and wrapped multiline text | `dxui.textinput.key_to_state_us`, `dxui.textinput.layout_us`, `dxui.textinput.visible_line_count` | `DxUiTests.exe --suite=NativeTextInput` plus existing `MultilineText` / `ReadOnly` |
| Pointer hit-test, drag selection, double/triple click | `dxui.textinput.hit_test_us`, `dxui.textinput.selection_update_us` | `DxUiTests.exe --suite=NativeTextInput` |
| LTR, RTL, and mixed BiDi editing | `dxui.textinput.bidi_hit_test_us`, `dxui.textinput.bidi_caret_rect_us`, `dxui.textinput.selection_update_us` | `DxUiTests.exe --suite=NativeTextInput`; `DxUiTests.exe --suite=Rendering` for visual snapshots if geometry tests are insufficient |
| Color emoji and extended grapheme clusters | `dxui.textinput.color_glyph_pixel_count`, `dxui.textinput.grapheme_step_us`, `dxui.textinput.selection_update_us` | `DxUiTests.exe --suite=NativeTextInput`; existing `DxUiTests.exe --suite=TextField` color-font pixel test |
| Password and secret entry | `dxui.textinput.secret_render_us`, `dxui.textinput.secret_display_dot_count`, `dxui.textinput.reveal_toggle_us` | `DxUiTests.exe --suite=NativeTextInput`; `DxUiTests.exe --suite=Accessibility`; credential prompt command selftests |
| Clipboard and undo/redo | `dxui.textinput.edit_transaction_us`, `dxui.textinput.undo_depth` | `DxUiTests.exe --suite=NativeTextInput` |
| IME start/update/result/cancel | `dxui.textinput.ime_update_us`, `dxui.textinput.composition_length`, `dxui.textinput.candidate_rect_update_us` | `DxUiTests.exe --suite=NativeTextInput` with IMM32 message simulation; manual IME smoke recorded if OS IME automation is blocked |
| UIA text range and value access | `dxui.uia.text_range_us`, `dxui.uia.text_setvalue_us` | `DxUiTests.exe --suite=Accessibility` |
| NavigationView address-bar editing | `navigation.textinput.enter_edit_us`, `navigation.textinput.key_to_suggest_us` | `RedSalamander.exe --commands-selftest --selftest-case=cmd_pane_navigationView_` plus address-bar clipboard case |
| Bridge removal audit | `audit.remaining_win32_ui.seconds` | `Tools/Audit-RemainingWin32UiDependencies.ps1 -FailOnFindings` with bridge allowlist removed |

- [x] Preserve one-dot-per-grapheme typing feedback in `Exact` mode. A pasted or typed grapheme cluster such as `👩‍💻`, a skin-tone emoji, or a regional-indicator flag must display as one dot, not one dot per UTF-16 code unit.
- [x] Implement `Concealed` length-hiding mode for high-sensitivity secrets. The displayed dot count must be independent of the exact secret length within configured buckets, stable during a focus/edit epoch to avoid distracting jitter, and regenerated only on focus entry, explicit full reset, mask/policy change, or bucket boundary crossing. Current slice verifies privacy display ranges, same-bucket typing stability, full-reset regeneration, refocus regeneration, and boundary-crossing regeneration.
- [x] In `Concealed` mode, define editing affordances explicitly: normal typing, paste, Backspace/Delete, Select All, full replacement, undo/redo, and end-caret movement are supported; middle-of-string pointer placement may either map through logical caret geometry or be disabled with a documented cursor/selection policy. The chosen behavior is documented as hidden single-line pointer end-snap with keyboard/command editing retained, and is covered by native tests.
- [x] Disable copy and cut only while the secret is not currently revealed. When the user intentionally reveals the text through `Peek` or `Visible`, Copy, Cut, and Paste are valid command/menu/keyboard actions; Cut must mutate the underlying secret exactly like ordinary text editing and then keep the field in the currently requested reveal state until the normal re-mask trigger fires. Clipboard paste remains allowed in both hidden and revealed states and normalizes text through the same native path as ordinary text input. Current slice verifies visible-state Ctrl+C/Ctrl+X mutation plus explicit `PasswordRevealMode::Visible` copy across blur/read-only/disabled transitions; context-menu and reveal-eye command surfaces remain open.
- [x] Ensure UIA exposes `IsPassword=true`, returns no secret through `ValuePattern::get_Value`, `TextPattern::GetText`, or text ranges while masked, and exposes the reveal button as a separate `Button`/`InvokePattern` named from a localized/configurable resource such as "Show password". - [x] Add keyboard accessibility for reveal without leaking value through accessibility: Tab can reach the reveal button when shown, Space/Enter press-and-hold follows the selected peek behavior, Shift+Tab returns to the field, the next Tab moves to the next focusable control, and blur/release re-mask.
- [x] Add tests for exact-dot rendering, concealed-dot rendering with non-equal visible count, stability of concealed count during ordinary typing inside a bucket, regeneration on a new focus epoch, one-dot-per-grapheme emoji masking, eye press/release reveal, keyboard reveal, blur re-mask, disabled/read-only re-mask, copy/cut suppression while hidden, copy/cut/paste allowed while revealed, Cut mutating the revealed secret and preserving undo, paste allowed while hidden, undo/redo, and selection/caret behavior under both length policies. ## Bridge-Era Behavior Inventory

The native replacement must preserve the behavior currently covered by 180 bridge-focused compatibility tests plus semantic behaviors already migrated into native suites:

- Focus and ownership: bridge creation, hidden bridge lifetime, focus transitions, blur on external focus loss, descendant-focus suppression, default/cancel button routing, Tab/Shift+Tab traversal, Menu/Shift+F10 context menu routing, and mixed dialog flow.
- Single-line editing: `WM_SETTEXT`, `WM_CHAR`, printable insertion, ignored tab characters, Return invoking default action, selection replacement, Ctrl+A, Home/End/arrow keys, Ctrl+word navigation, Ctrl+Backspace/Delete, path-segment deletion, surrogate-pair deletion, copy/cut/paste accelerators, no-selection clipboard preservation, undo/redo, and editable `ComboBox` parity.
- Multiline and wrapped multiline editing: logical newline normalization, CRLF bridge mapping, selection import/export, replacement across logical newlines, Return inserting newline without invoking default action, viewport synchronization, scroll/wheel behavior, visual-line movement for wrapped text, page movement, and preserving visible caret/selection after layout changes.
- Clipboard and edit transactions: Ctrl+C/Ctrl+Insert, Ctrl+X/Shift+Delete, Ctrl+V/Shift+Insert, clear selection, no-selection clipboard preservation, partial and newline-spanning selections, paste normalization, undo/redo restoring text and selection, and redo clearing after new edits.
- IME and geometry: composition ownership of special keys, result commit ownership, composition/result transitions for single-line/multiline/wrapped fields, IME candidate/composition windows tracking caret rects, moved-control bounds, and wrapped-line caret positions.
- Secret and read-only behavior: masked text accepts character input, hidden masked state suppresses copy/cut, paste remains valid, and read-only paths suppress mutations while retaining safe navigation/selection.
- Navigation-specific policy: address-bar edit-mode, full-path popup edit route, autosuggest keyboard routing, clipboard accelerators, Enter/Escape/Tab behavior, invalid-path feedback, and pane-level command routing.

## Open Risks

- TSF implementation remains high-risk because COM locking, reentrancy, screen-coordinate geometry, and selection notification rules are easy to get subtly wrong. The bridge-removal gate has direct deterministic coverage, but future text-store hardening should continue to be small and test-first.
- Some IME behavior may not be fully automatable in CI. Deterministic IMM32/TSF coverage is now the closeout gate; manual IME smoke remains additional release QA.
- Bridge HWND debug snapshot contracts have been retired from production/default behavior. Remaining bridge mentions are historical evidence or the negative sentinel helper used to prove no hidden child is created.
- UIA `TextPattern` / `TextEditPattern` support is now the native edit-control contract. Future range-geometry polish is ordinary hardening, not a blocker for deleting the bridge.
- Password UX has security and usability tradeoffs. Exact one-dot-per-grapheme remains the default for normal password entry; concealed length-hiding mode must be explicit and documented because it can make mid-string editing less intuitive.
- BiDi is not just text rendering. The native path must prove logical UTF-16 indexes, visual DirectWrite geometry, keyboard editing, TSF ACP mapping, IME candidate placement, and UIA range rectangles agree for LTR, RTL, and mixed-script text.
- Color emoji is also not just surrogate-pair safety. Native input must preserve DirectWrite color-font drawing and user-visible grapheme behavior for ZWJ sequences, variation selectors, skin-tone modifiers, and flags.
- Performance can regress if native mode recreates DirectWrite layouts per message. Cache layout/hit-test data and emit metrics before claiming parity.

## Self-Review

- Spec coverage: The plan covers bridge removal, native focus/key input, clipboard/edit history, password/secret entry, LTR/RTL/mixed-BiDi, color emoji/grapheme behavior, IME, TSF, UIA, NavigationView policy, editable combo consumers, audits, performance, and closeout specs.
- Placeholder scan: No `TBD` / `TODO` placeholders remain; blocked cases require exact blockers to be recorded during execution.
- Scope check: This is intentionally one subsystem plan: DxUi text input and direct consumers. Broader popup/menu/native-bitmap interop remains outside this plan except where audits prove bridge removal.
- Evidence gate: Production bridge deletion is complete; baseline, candidate, correctness, perf, audit, command, and spec-closeout gates are recorded above.
